### 理解測驗

1. **Why does the first event matter?**
   - A. Hale attempts to activate the midnight update despite the court order, using a backup data center.
   - B. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   - C. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 黑爾試圖透過備援資料中心違反法院命令啟動午夜更新。

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. Ren and the engineers preserve the update log while the tribunal orders a safe system-wide pause.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 任與工程師保全更新紀錄，法庭下令安全地全面暫停。


## CHAPTER 21｜The Right to Decline｜拒絕的權利

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：859**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The first sign of trouble was not an explosion or a scream. It was a familiar procedure producing a result nobody could defend. The court voids the clause that treats silence as approval and establishes an independent consent standard. Ren checked the time, the witnesses, and the rule that authorized the action. The three did not agree. Outside, ordinary life continued, which made the error more frightening rather than less.

**繁中** 麻煩的第一個徵兆不是爆炸或尖叫，而是熟悉程序得出了誰也無法辯護的結果。法院撤銷把沉默當同意的條款，確立獨立的同意標準。任核對時間、證人與授權規則，三者卻互相矛盾。外頭的平凡生活照常進行，反而讓這個錯誤更加可怕。

**EN** An automated door opened before Ren could finish asking whether she wanted to enter. The machine congratulated her on an efficient choice. Davi stood still beside the threshold. Efficiency, he said, was meaningless if the person standing in front of the door had never been allowed to answer.

**繁中** 任還沒問完自己是否想進去，自動門便打開了。機器恭喜她做出有效率的選擇。戴維停在門檻旁說，如果站在門前的人從未被允許回答，效率就沒有意義。

**EN** A witness asked whether the inquiry would make tomorrow safer. Ren could not promise that. The truth could establish responsibility, but it would not repair a broken machine, feed a hungry child, or return lost time on its own. Davi suggested a temporary protection that could be checked after sunrise. They wrote down its limits in plain language and invited the people affected to challenge them.

**繁中** 一位證人問調查是否能讓明天更安全，任無法保證。真相能確立責任，卻不會自行修好機器、餵飽孩子或歸還失去的時間。戴維提出一項日出後可以檢驗的暫時保護措施。他們用白話寫清限制，邀請受影響的人提出異議。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The new charter made every board member accountable for documented decisions. Ren used an analogy of a damaged library book to explain why restoration could not guarantee a perfect memory. The public auditorium hosted a debate over how many institutions should share custody of the archives. A cardinal rule of the audit was to preserve every original record before asking witnesses to interpret it. The nurse remained communicative throughout the procedure so the volunteer could understand each step. Ren asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 新章程要求每位董事對有紀錄的決定負責。 任以受損圖書作比喻，說明恢復不保證得到完美記憶。 公共禮堂舉辦辯論，討論幾個機構應共同保管檔案。 稽核最重要的一項原則，是先保存所有原始紀錄，再請證人解釋。 護理師在整個療程中持續清楚溝通，讓志願者理解每個步驟。 任要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** For once the numbers did not merely decorate a report; they contradicted the order that had been issued in their name. Hale’s unlawful changes go to separate investigation; public services continue with manual alternatives. Davi asked whether a mistake could explain the result. Ren agreed to test that possibility too. They checked an independent source, marked every assumption, and made copies before the originals could be transferred to an office beyond public reach.

**繁中** 這一次數字不只是報告裝飾，而是直接反駁以數字名義發布的命令。黑爾違法改動另案調查，公共服務則以手動替代方案繼續。戴維詢問是否可能只是出錯，任也同意檢查這種可能。他們核對獨立來源、標記每項假設，在原件被移往公眾無法接觸的辦公室前留下副本。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Nari called the elderly teacher a comrade in reform without expecting her to reveal her private memories. A volunteer reported a hand cramp during testing, and the clinician immediately paused the session. The protocol required doctors to hospitalize any participant with serious adverse reactions rather than hide the event. The new uniform caused an itch for one cleaner, so the employer supplied an alternative. Lan said longevity mattered less to her than having a meaningful daily routine. Davi marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 娜莉稱年老教師是改革夥伴，卻不要求她揭露私人記憶。 一名志願者在測試時手部抽筋，醫師立刻暫停療程。 程序要求醫師遇到嚴重不良反應時安排住院，而非隱瞞事件。 新制服讓一名清潔員皮膚發癢，雇主因此提供替代款。 婉如說，對她而言，長壽不如每天過得有意義。 戴維標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Administrator Hale and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Ren rejected both. The issue was whether anyone may choose to say no after an algorithm has chosen for them, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住黑爾管理官的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。任兩者都拒絕。核心是演算法替人選擇後，當事人是否仍有權說不，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The teacher's decision to stop helped propel the clinic toward a stronger culture of consent. A student wrote a piece of prose about learning to enter the water. The clinician rejected the claim that patients had gained psychic powers merely because they described similar dreams. An independent referee reviewed disputes between archive custodians and residents requesting access. Years of relentless overnight correction had left some patients unsure which memories were their own. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 教師決定停止療程，推動診所建立更強的同意文化。 一名學生寫了篇散文，描述學會入水的經驗。 臨床醫師拒絕說病患因描述相似夢境就獲得通靈能力。 一名獨立仲裁員審查檔案保管機構與申請查閱居民之間的爭議。 多年不停的夜間修正，使部分病患不確定哪些記憶屬於自己。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** An automated door opened before Ren could finish asking whether she wanted to enter. The machine congratulated her on an efficient choice. Davi stood still beside the threshold. Efficiency, he said, was meaningless if the person standing in front of the door had never been allowed to answer.

**繁中** 任還沒問完自己是否想進去，自動門便打開了。機器恭喜她做出有效率的選擇。戴維停在門檻旁說，如果站在門前的人從未被允許回答，效率就沒有意義。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A paper train ticket became a cherished relic, not proof that every passenger on its route had survived. Workers had to scrape loose paint away before applying the new protective coating. Visitors could scroll through public summaries without gaining access to anyone's sealed medical file. A child's red sneaker remained in a sealed evidence box because its owner had not consented to display. Officials had continued to underestimate residents' capacity to make informed decisions about their own memories. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 一張紙本車票成為珍貴遺物，卻不能證明路線上每名乘客都活著。 工人塗上新保護層前，必須刮除鬆動油漆。 訪客可以捲動閱讀公開摘要，卻不能看到任何人的密封病歷。 一隻兒童紅球鞋仍封存在證物箱裡，因為物主尚未同意展示。 官員一直低估居民對自身記憶做出知情選擇的能力。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Ren had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Davi wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Administrator Hale. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。任知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。戴維把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起黑爾管理官注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

### 理解測驗

1. **What unexpected event begins this chapter?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The court voids the clause that treats silence as approval and establishes an independent consent standard.
   - C. The authorities announce a festival unrelated to the crisis.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 法院撤銷把沉默當同意的條款，確立獨立的同意標準。

2. **What does the later discovery establish?**
   - A. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - B. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - C. Hale’s unlawful changes go to separate investigation; public services continue with manual alternatives.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 黑爾違法改動另案調查，公共服務則以手動替代方案繼續。


## CHAPTER 22｜The New Interface｜新的介面

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Morning brought a decision that had already been made for everyone. There was a signature at the bottom of the order, but no space for those most affected to answer. Citizens receive clear choices with explainable consequences, accessible controls, and the option to delay a decision. Ren knew that refusing a bad choice was not the same as knowing the correct one. Before making a promise, the team needed something they could test.

**繁中** 清晨帶來一項替所有人做好的決定。命令底下有簽名，卻沒有讓受影響最深者回答的地方。公民獲得清楚的選項、可理解的後果、無障礙控制及延後決定的權利。任知道拒絕壞選項不等於已經知道正確答案。作出承諾前，團隊需要能夠驗證的事物。

**EN** An automated door opened before Ren could finish asking whether she wanted to enter. The machine congratulated her on an efficient choice. Davi stood still beside the threshold. Efficiency, he said, was meaningless if the person standing in front of the door had never been allowed to answer.

**繁中** 任還沒問完自己是否想進去，自動門便打開了。機器恭喜她做出有效率的選擇。戴維停在門檻旁說，如果站在門前的人從未被允許回答，效率就沒有意義。

**EN** The evidence did not arrive as a confession. It came through a date that did not fit, a measurement that had been repeated, and a witness who knew exactly what the official account had left out. Ren asked Davi not to describe suspicion as proof. If the accusation was wrong, an innocent person would suffer. If it was right, careless language could still give the guilty a way to escape.

**繁中** 證據不是以自白形式出現，而是一個對不上的日期、一項重複測量的結果，以及一位知道官方說法遺漏何事的證人。任請戴維不要把懷疑說成證明。若指控錯了，無辜者會受害；若指控正確，草率措辭仍可能讓有罪者脫身。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The archive contained an abundance of handwritten logs, though only a fraction had yet been carefully cataloged. The guardian asked the team to avoid causing unnecessary distress to survivors named in the recovered files. A resident's fury about hidden repair costs was understandable, but the meeting required precise questions rather than threats. A child's old drawing of a gorilla helped identify the school from which one evacuation group had departed. The disputed case archive also contained smaller, testable observations:  The revised bus schedule could hamper the evening access program. Davi marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 檔案室有大量手寫日誌，但目前只有一小部分完成仔細編目。 監護人要求小組避免讓檔案上出現的倖存者承受不必要痛苦。 居民對隱藏修繕費用的憤怒可以理解，會議仍要求精確提問而非威脅。 一張兒童大猩猩舊畫，協助辨認某批撤離者出發的學校。 有爭議的案件檔案還包含一些可檢驗的小細節： 修訂公車時刻表可能妨礙晚間無障礙課程。 戴維標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A small act of honesty unsettled the room more than a shouted threat could have done. The AI remains useful because its predictions can be declined and challenged. Ren watched a witness decide not to change a difficult detail merely because it complicated the preferred story. Davi recorded the correction openly. Their inquiry would be slower now, but its shape belonged less to the powerful and more to the people who had lived through the event.

**繁中** 一個小小的誠實舉動，帶來比喊叫威脅更強的震動。AI 仍然有用，正因為它的預測可以被拒絕與質疑。任看見證人沒有因為細節讓主流說法變複雜就改口。戴維公開記錄修正。調查會因此變慢，卻能較少受權貴支配，也更貼近親歷者。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: A harmonica player entertained adults waiting for the transport meeting to begin. The archive's privacy policy protected heterosexual and LGBTQ families equally when recording relationships. The slow audit could irritate impatient activists, but it reduced the risk of accusing innocent workers. Some witnesses chose to remain mute in public and submit private written testimony instead. The forged epidemic order appeared to originate from a council office that had controlled the first relocations. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 一名口琴演奏者在交通會議開始前為等候成人演奏。 檔案隱私政策在記錄親密關係時，同等保障異性戀與 LGBTQ 家庭。 緩慢稽核可能使焦急倡議者不耐煩，卻降低誤控無辜工人的風險。 部分證人選擇在公開場合保持沉默，改交私人書面證詞。 偽造的疫情命令似乎源自控制第一批搬遷的議會辦公室。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** No one in the room was permitted to declare that a good intention canceled a harmful action. Ren insisted on recording the error, identifying who had authorized it, and protecting those affected before assigning wider blame. Davi drew a simple boundary around what they could do tonight. The line looked disappointingly modest, yet it was the first promise that did not require another person to surrender a right.

**繁中** 室內沒有人能宣稱善意可以抵銷傷害。任堅持記錄錯誤、確認授權者，並在追究更大責任前保護受害者。戴維畫出今晚能做到的清楚界線。這條線看似微不足道，卻是第一個不要求別人放棄權利的承諾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: The disputed case archive also contained smaller, testable observations:  A child pointed to a pony in the nearby fairground photograph. Vale's predecessor had signed some secrecy orders, but the investigation separated their individual decisions. Residents could preview redacted archive summaries before deciding whether to request their own files. The original departure ledger was priceless to families seeking an accurate account of their relatives. Archivists worked to salvage water-damaged letters without replacing missing words with guesses. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 有爭議的案件檔案還包含一些可檢驗的小細節： 一名孩子指著附近園遊會照片中的小馬。 維爾前任簽過部分保密命令，調查仍分開審查兩人的個別決定。 居民可先預覽刪去敏感資訊的檔案摘要，再決定是否申請自己的紀錄。 對尋求親人真實去向的家庭而言，原始出發名冊無價。 檔案員努力搶救受潮書信，沒有用猜測補上缺失文字。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** An automated door opened before Ren could finish asking whether she wanted to enter. The machine congratulated her on an efficient choice. Davi stood still beside the threshold. Efficiency, he said, was meaningless if the person standing in front of the door had never been allowed to answer.

**繁中** 任還沒問完自己是否想進去，自動門便打開了。機器恭喜她做出有效率的選擇。戴維停在門檻旁說，如果站在門前的人從未被允許回答，效率就沒有意義。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: A sneaky change to the archive index had concealed the vault's location for decades. The archive allowed donors to choose whether their surname appeared beside a public description of family photographs. A sudden torrent of rain flooded the service stair, forcing the team to suspend the archive transfer. A retired veterinarian identified animal transport stamps on a set of railway cargo records. A young violinist performed at the meeting's close, after trustees had recorded the difficult budget decisions in full. Ren asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 一項偷偷修改檔案索引的行為，使地庫位置被藏了幾十年。 檔案室讓捐贈者選擇家族照片公開說明旁是否顯示姓氏。 一陣暴雨急流淹過維修樓梯，迫使小組暫停檔案移交。 退休獸醫認出一批鐵路貨運紀錄上的動物運輸印章。 受託人完整記錄困難預算決議後，一名年輕小提琴家在會末演奏。 任要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** On the way out, Ren passed people waiting for a result that would affect their meals, their homes, or their ability to say no. None of them had the luxury of treating the dispute as an intellectual puzzle. Davi paused to answer a child's practical question before they left. The answer was incomplete, but unlike the old official notice, it admitted the uncertainty out loud.

**繁中** 離開時，任經過等待結果的人群，這項結果將影響他們的餐食、家園或說不的能力。他們不能奢侈地把爭論當成智力謎題。戴維停下回答一名孩子實際的問題。答案並不完整，卻與舊公告不同：它坦白承認不確定之處。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. Citizens receive clear choices with explainable consequences, accessible controls, and the option to delay a decision.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 公民獲得清楚的選項、可理解的後果、無障礙控制及延後決定的權利。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. The AI remains useful because its predictions can be declined and challenged.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** AI 仍然有用，正因為它的預測可以被拒絕與質疑。


## CHAPTER 23｜A Classroom of Questions｜提問的教室

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：838**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** By noon, rumors had outrun the facts. Some people wanted a villain; others wanted a hero who could end the argument without asking another question. Davi teaches children to ask why a recommendation was made before they accept it. Ren had worked long enough to distrust both shortcuts. In the automated metropolis, the first task was to protect the evidence before anyone could turn it into a weapon.

**繁中** 到了中午，謠言早已跑得比事實快。有人想找反派，也有人想找英雄，好在不再追問的情況下結束爭論。戴維教孩子在接受建議前先問為什麼。任工作多年，已經不相信這兩種捷徑。在全自動都會，當務之急是先保護證據，免得任何人將它變成武器。

**EN** An automated door opened before Ren could finish asking whether she wanted to enter. The machine congratulated her on an efficient choice. Davi stood still beside the threshold. Efficiency, he said, was meaningless if the person standing in front of the door had never been allowed to answer.

**繁中** 任還沒問完自己是否想進去，自動門便打開了。機器恭喜她做出有效率的選擇。戴維停在門檻旁說，如果站在門前的人從未被允許回答，效率就沒有意義。

**EN** Beyond the meeting room, people were already finding ways to survive. They traded tools, shared the location of safe passages, and argued over fair turns without waiting for permission. Ren listened longer than the schedule allowed. Davi reminded the officials that no policy was complete until it made sense to the people expected to live with it. Their notes began with those ordinary acts of cooperation.

**繁中** 會議室外，人們早已開始尋求生存方法：交換工具、分享安全路線的位置，無須等候許可便爭論公平輪班。任聽得比議程允許的更久。戴維提醒官員，政策若不能讓實際生活其中的人理解，就不算完整。他們從這些平凡合作開始記錄。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The court separated the railway collision reports from later claims that every missing person had died. The school planetarium announced a comet display on the same weekend as the meeting. The published inspection thresholds were meant to deter unsafe shortcuts, not punish a company for reporting an honest failure. One eccentric headline made the pool sound like a scene from a romance serial. Court officers would escort endangered witnesses between the hearing room and protected accommodation. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 法院把鐵路碰撞事故報告，與後來宣稱所有失蹤者都死亡的說法分開。 學校星象館宣布在會議同週末舉辦彗星展示。 公開檢查門檻旨在遏止不安全捷徑，而非懲罰誠實通報故障的公司。 一則古怪標題把泳池寫得像愛情連續劇場景。 法院人員會護送受威脅證人往返聽證室與保護住所。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** Then the next piece of evidence changed the shape of the case. One student changes the AI’s lesson plan after presenting evidence the model overlooked. Ren read the statement twice, searching for the sentence that would make the contradiction disappear. There was none. Davi did not reach for a dramatic accusation; instead, they listed three people who could verify the finding separately. The delay would be unpopular, but a dramatic discovery deserved more care, not less.

**繁中** 接著出現的新證據改變了整起案件的輪廓。一名學生提出模型忽略的證據，成功改變 AI 教案。任把陳述讀了兩遍，試圖找出能消除矛盾的句子，卻沒有找到。戴維沒有立刻提出轟動指控，而是列出三位可分別驗證發現的人。延遲會不受歡迎，但重大發現更需要謹慎。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: An engineering examinee used the case to explain why safety reviews must include deployment conditions. A child's garment retained the stitched name of a school removed from official maps. The transport authority required a full navigation and track safety review before reopening the coastal route. Ning's outlook changed from heroic rescue to the slower work of institutions that could be challenged. A surviving paperback novel contained a handwritten address from the vanished orchard district. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**EN** The debate sharpened when Administrator Hale demanded a decision before the evidence could be copied. Ren replied that urgency and secrecy were not the same thing. They established a temporary limit, published the reason for it, and scheduled an independent review. The result satisfied nobody completely. That was acceptable; the purpose of the process was to make abuse harder, not to produce applause.

A sorrowful account of a past shipwreck reminded the board why disputed safety records deserved serious attention. The old correction policy had created a spiral of fear, secrecy, and further technical intervention. Ren asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**EN** An automated door opened before Ren could finish asking whether she wanted to enter. The machine congratulated her on an efficient choice. Davi stood still beside the threshold. Efficiency, he said, was meaningless if the person standing in front of the door had never been allowed to answer.

**繁中** 任還沒問完自己是否想進去，自動門便打開了。機器恭喜她做出有效率的選擇。戴維停在門檻旁說，如果站在門前的人從未被允許回答，效率就沒有意義。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: No subordinate should be ordered to falsify a medical record, regardless of who holds authority. The new regulations forbade operators to transmit memory-altering pulses without individualized medical consent. The artificial stars continued to twinkle above the courthouse after the ruling was announced. An old velvet seat from the missing train was preserved as evidence, not turned into a propaganda display. A nearby machine continued to vibrate until its operator switched it off. Davi marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 不論上級權勢多大，都不能命令下屬偽造病歷。 新規禁止未取得個別醫療同意就傳送改變記憶的脈衝。 判決宣布後，人造星星仍在法院上方閃爍。 一張失蹤列車上的舊天鵝絨座椅被保留為證物，而非宣傳展示。 附近一台機器持續震動，直到操作員將它關閉。 戴維標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** A message arrived after the public doors had closed. It carried no grand declaration, just a name and a time that did not match the official schedule. Ren resisted the urge to call it proof. Davi opened a fresh page in the journal and recorded the discrepancy exactly. They had won time, not certainty, and there were still people who might lose everything if they hurried.

**繁中** 公共大門關閉後，一則訊息送到。它沒有豪言壯語，只有一個名字和一個對不上官方時程的時間。任克制住把它當成證明的衝動。戴維翻開日誌新的一頁，逐字記錄差異。他們贏得的是時間，不是確定性；若急於求成，仍有人可能失去一切。

   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.

2. **Which finding changes the characters’ understanding?**
   - A. The evidence proves that no public decision needs review.
   - B. One student changes the AI’s lesson plan after presenting evidence the model overlooked.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：B。繁中解析：** 一名學生提出模型忽略的證據，成功改變 AI 教案。

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** The room smelled of dust, hot metal, and rain carried in on people's coats. Someone had left an ordinary object on the central table, yet no one wanted to touch it. Ren restores her mother’s closed account and removes the fraudulent renewal from the public record. Ren saw that the object mattered less for what it was than for the decision it proved had been made. There would be no honest way back to ignorance.

**繁中** 室內有灰塵、熱金屬與人們外套帶進的雨味。有人把平凡物件放在中央桌上，卻沒有人願意碰它。任恢復母親已結束的帳戶狀態，刪除公開紀錄上的虛假續約。任明白重要的不是物件本身，而是它證明了何種決定已經發生。如今已不可能誠實地假裝無知。

**EN** An automated door opened before Ren could finish asking whether she wanted to enter. The machine congratulated her on an efficient choice. Davi stood still beside the threshold. Efficiency, he said, was meaningless if the person standing in front of the door had never been allowed to answer.

**繁中** 任還沒問完自己是否想進去，自動門便打開了。機器恭喜她做出有效率的選擇。戴維停在門檻旁說，如果站在門前的人從未被允許回答，效率就沒有意義。

**EN** Davi arrived with a second account that neither confirmed nor dismissed the first. They compared the routes by which each record had reached the room, then placed the uncertainties beside the facts. Ren could feel the pressure to announce a solution before anyone became frightened. That pressure was precisely why a second witness mattered: a conclusion that could not survive an independent question would be dangerous, even if it happened to be right.

**繁中** 戴維帶來第二份說法，既未證實也未推翻第一份。兩人比對紀錄抵達房間的途徑，再把不確定之處和事實並列。任感受到眾人希望立即宣布解方，以免恐懼擴大的壓力。但也正因這種壓力，第二位證人才重要：無法通過獨立質問的結論，即使碰巧正確，也可能很危險。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The real coastline extended beyond the walls farther than any public school map had shown. The new director would compile public reports from several village committees. The independent bridge inspection would complement the medical safeguards for family travelers. Ren did not try to console Ren with promises that the lost years could simply be recovered. The new travel charter barred officials from using forgotten family records to detain innocent passengers. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 真正的海岸線延伸到城牆外，比任何公立學校地圖畫得更遠。 新主任將彙整數個村委會的公開報告。 獨立橋梁檢查將補足探親旅客的醫療保障。 任沒有試圖用「失去歲月能輕易找回」的承諾安慰梅。 新旅行章程禁止官員利用被遺忘的家屬紀錄拘留無辜乘客。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The contradiction reached the table just as the meeting was about to end. She refuses an honorary position offered by Hale’s former company and chooses independent advocacy. For a long moment no one spoke. Ren understood why silence felt comfortable; each answer threatened someone's settled account of how the world worked. Davi broke the stillness by asking who would be harmed if they simply filed the discovery away. No one could answer without naming a real person.

**繁中** 會議即將結束時，矛盾證據送上桌面。她拒絕黑爾舊公司提供的榮譽職位，選擇獨立倡議。一時之間無人開口。任明白沉默為何讓人舒服：任何答案都可能動搖某人對世界的既定理解。戴維打破沉默，問若把發現歸檔，究竟誰會受害。沒人能在不指名真實人物的情況下回答。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Diplomacy between Everday and Court Nine made the first supervised passenger service possible. The audit traced a downward adjustment in the reported repair costs. the office council adopted drastic changes only after independent review. The authorities avoided a drastic reopening before the damaged bridges passed independent tests. The coastal museum displayed a glacier survey that proved the outside world still had active scientific institutions. Ren refused to treat the new coastal reports as gospel; they too required verification and public challenge. Ren asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 永晝與九號港之間的外交協商，使首班受監督客運成為可能。 辦公場所議會經獨立審查後才採取劇烈改革。 當局避免在受損橋梁通過獨立檢查前，進行激烈全面開放。 海岸博物館展示一份冰河測量，證明外部世界仍有運作中的科學機構。 任拒絕把新的海岸報告當成絕對真理，它們也需要驗證與公開質疑。 任要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** Ren had expected the hardest part to be exposing the secret. Instead, it was choosing what to do after a partial truth became public. The people who had been ignored did not all want the same remedy. Davi proposed that each affected group could submit a written objection and receive a specific answer. The method would take time, but it recognized them as participants rather than scenery.

**繁中** 任原以為最難的是揭露祕密，後來才發現，部分真相公開後如何行動才是難題。曾被忽略的人並不全想要相同補救。戴維提議每個受影響群體都能提出書面異議並取得具體答覆。方法需要時間，但終於把他們當成參與者，而不是背景。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: Security staff had procedures to prevent anyone from attempting to hijack the first passenger train. Ning admitted she would be a hypocrite if she demanded transparent records while hiding her own testing mistakes. A familiar station jingle played as the repaired train approached Harbor Nine. Ivo admitted he had felt lonesome for years, though he did not expect Ren to repair that loss for him. The new map printed accurate longitude markings for towns once erased from public navigation systems. Davi marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 保全人員有程序，防止有人企圖劫持首班客車。 寧澄承認，若要求別人透明、自己卻藏起測試錯誤，就是偽善者。 修復列車接近九號港時，響起熟悉的車站短音樂。 伊沃承認多年來感到孤單，卻不期待任替他修復所有失落。 新地圖為昔日從公共導航系統抹除的城鎮印上準確經度標記。 戴維標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** An automated door opened before Ren could finish asking whether she wanted to enter. The machine congratulated her on an efficient choice. Davi stood still beside the threshold. Efficiency, he said, was meaningless if the person standing in front of the door had never been allowed to answer.

**繁中** 任還沒問完自己是否想進去，自動門便打開了。機器恭喜她做出有效率的選擇。戴維停在門檻旁說，如果站在門前的人從未被允許回答，效率就沒有意義。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The storm had once produced monstrous waves here, but the new chart described the hazards in measured terms. The reopening ceremony acknowledged the oppression that had kept families separated for decades. The newly surveyed reef remained marked on the chart even after the navigation beacon passed its tests. The two cities promised solidarity through practical rights, not a forced declaration of shared identity. The new commission could not become another vice gripping citizens' private memories in the name of safety. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 這裡曾出現可怕巨浪，新海圖卻以可衡量方式描述危險。 重開儀式承認過去壓迫如何使家庭分離數十年。 即使導航燈通過測試，新測礁石仍保留標示在海圖上。 兩座城市承諾以實際權利展現團結，而非強迫宣告共同身分。 新委員會不能再以安全之名，成為箝制居民私人記憶的惡習與束縛。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** When the lamps were lowered, Ren returned to a red refusal key. It was neither proof of innocence nor a shortcut to justice. It was a reminder that the next decision would be witnessed by people who no longer trusted easy promises. Davi remained at the door while the final record was sealed. Outside, the automated metropolis waited for news that had to be more precise than a victory speech.

**繁中** 燈光調暗時，任再次看向一枚紅色拒絕鍵。它既不是清白證明，也不是通往正義的捷徑，而是在提醒眾人：下一個決定會由不再相信輕易承諾的人見證。戴維守在門邊，最後一份紀錄被封存。外頭的全自動都會等待的消息，必須比勝利演說更精確。

## CHAPTER 25｜The Quietest Choice｜最安靜的選擇

**英文正文詞數（程式計算）：898**

### 正文與翻譯

**EN** Nobody had called it a crisis at first. A clerk had used a harmless word, a guard had followed an ordinary instruction, and by evening somebody else was carrying the cost. Ren refused to begin with the official conclusion. At midnight, Ren receives a suggestion from her personal AI and simply declines it. In the automated metropolis, even an accurate number could become a lie when it left out the person whose life it counted.

**繁中** 起初沒有人稱這是危機。書記使用看似無害的詞，守衛照普通命令行事，到了晚上代價卻落在別人身上。任拒絕從官方結論開始。午夜時，任收到個人 AI 的建議，平靜地按下拒絕。在全自動都會，精確的數字若遺漏真正承受後果的人，也可能成為謊言。

**EN** An automated door opened before Ren could finish asking whether she wanted to enter. The machine congratulated her on an efficient choice. Davi stood still beside the threshold. Efficiency, he said, was meaningless if the person standing in front of the door had never been allowed to answer.

**繁中** 任還沒問完自己是否想進去，自動門便打開了。機器恭喜她做出有效率的選擇。戴維停在門檻旁說，如果站在門前的人從未被允許回答，效率就沒有意義。

**EN** The argument was not merely about who held authority. It was about what happened when an official decision met a household, a workplace, or a body that could not obey it safely. Ren asked Davi to describe the smallest practical consequence. Only then did they realize that the proposed remedy would reproduce the very harm it claimed to prevent. They changed the plan before presenting it in public.

**繁中** 爭論不只是誰有權威，而是官方決定落到家庭、職場或無法安全服從的身體上時會發生什麼。任請戴維說明最小、最實際的後果。他們這才發現提議中的補救會重複它聲稱要防止的傷害，因此在公開發表前修改了計畫。

**EN** The overlooked details in the witness file made the question harder, not easier: The new clinic accommodated residents with asthma who needed careful respiratory monitoring during sleep treatment. A bilateral agreement between Everday and the local station protected voluntary family visits and data privacy. Nari worked with her coastal counterpart to coordinate independent reviews of shared transport records. The coastal train used a backup diesel engine during sections where the electric line remained under repair. The clinic stopped using disposable sensor bands when reusable alternatives passed medical safety tests. Ren asked the clerk to preserve the original wording rather than improve the story.

**繁中** 證人檔案裡被忽視的細節使問題更加複雜，而不是更簡單： 新診所照顧有氣喘的居民，在睡眠治療期間仔細監測呼吸。 歷史修正局與九號港的雙邊協議，保障自願探親與資料隱私。 娜莉與海岸地區對口人員合作，協調共同運輸紀錄的獨立審查。 海岸列車行經電線仍在維修的路段時，使用備援柴油引擎。 可重複使用的感測環通過醫療安全測試後，診所不再使用拋棄式環帶。 任要求書記保存原本措辭，而不是把故事修飾得更好聽。

**EN** What changed the discussion was a fact that should have been obvious from the start. The machine answers understood, and no door locks, account closes, or future disappears. Ren felt the old temptation to turn that fact into an immediate victory. Instead, the team separated what the new evidence established from what it merely suggested. That distinction frustrated the loudest spectators, but it helped the witnesses remain willing to speak again.

**繁中** 改變討論的是一件本來就該看得見的事實。機器回答已理解，沒有門鎖上、沒有帳戶關閉，也沒有任何未來消失。任一瞬間也想把它當成立刻勝利的證明，團隊卻把新證據真正確立的部分與僅能推測的部分分開。這讓最喧鬧的旁觀者失望，卻使證人願意再次出面。

**EN** A second bundle of observations brought the consequences closer to ordinary life: Officials could no longer divert requests for missing-person records into a general category of sleep complaints. A tired lad in the beginners' group began to doze before the evening briefing. A friend joked that Ivo was Ren's fiancé, but Ren replied that they had made no such announcement. The archive trust depended on enforceable rules as well as goodwill among former opponents. The new archive used a hybrid system of physical originals and verified digital copies. Davi marked what could be verified and left the rest open to challenge.

**繁中** 第二疊觀察紀錄使後果貼近普通人的生活： 官員不能再把尋找失蹤者紀錄的申請，轉送到一般睡眠申訴類別。 初學團體一名疲憊少年在晚間簡報前開始打瞌睡。 朋友開玩笑稱伊沃是任未婚夫，任回答兩人從未宣布訂婚。 檔案信託除了仰賴昔日對手的善意，也需要可執行的規則。 新檔案館採用紙本正本與可驗證數位副本的混合制度。 戴維標記能查證的部分，其餘保留給人質疑。

**EN** There were two tempting shortcuts. One would protect the reputation of Administrator Hale and leave the public guessing. The other would expose every private detail at once and call that transparency. Ren rejected both. The issue was whether anyone may choose to say no after an algorithm has chosen for them, not whether the team could win an argument in a single afternoon. A smaller step, supported by independent observers, could protect people while the wider dispute remained open.

**繁中** 有兩種誘人的捷徑：一種保住黑爾管理官的名聲，讓大眾自行猜測；另一種把所有私人細節一次揭露，並稱之為透明。任兩者都拒絕。核心是演算法替人選擇後，當事人是否仍有權說不，不是團隊能否在一個下午贏得爭論。由獨立觀察者支持的一小步，即使爭議未結，也能先保護民眾。

**EN** The team kept the concrete observations alongside the disputed theory: An old lad from Orchard East returned as an elderly man to see the street restored to the map. The refurbished pool lining was checked after a full season of use. A handwritten manuscript of the neighborhood's history was added to the public library with its author's consent. Public outrage helped start the inquiry, but patient verification made the reforms durable. The paradox was that a smaller but safer route now reached more people. Nothing in these records excused the people who had made the dangerous decision.

**繁中** 團隊把具體觀察與有爭議的理論並列保存： 一名東果園昔日的少年如今已是老人，回來看街道重新出現在地圖上。 整修後的池壁內襯經過一整個使用季再次檢查。 一份手寫社區歷史稿在作者同意下納入公共圖書館。 公眾憤怒促成調查，耐心驗證才讓改革得以長久。 矛盾之處在於規模較小卻更安全的路線反而接觸更多人。 紀錄中沒有任何一項能替做出危險決定的人開脫。

**EN** The record continued with small facts that would be lost in a confident speech: The town helped rehabilitate the old weather classroom so future keepers could train without closing the public path. A renowned historian helped verify the original railway dates but did not control the public archive. Residents chose to revive the orchard festival with new traditions rather than recreate an imagined perfect past. Underneath the new public map lay layers of streets whose histories the city finally allowed people to study. The agreement rejected forced unification of the cities and protected each community's self-government. A coastal villa once used by senior officials was converted into a public center for reunion counseling. Taken alone, no detail settled the case; together they showed why a public answer mattered.

**繁中** 紀錄接著列出那些在自信演說中容易消失的小事實： 小鎮協助修復舊氣象教室，讓未來守護員不必封閉公共步道也能受訓。 一位著名歷史學家協助核實原始鐵路日期，卻不掌控公共檔案館。 居民選擇以新傳統恢復果園節，而不是重建想像中完美的過去。 新公共地圖底下有一層又一層街道歷史，如今城市終於允許人們研究。 協議拒絕強制統一兩座城市，保障各社區自治。 一棟曾供高階官員使用的海岸別墅，改成公共家屬重逢諮詢中心。 單獨看來沒有一項細節能結案，合在一起卻說明了公開答覆為何重要。

**EN** The chapter ended without an easy reassurance. Ren had learned one thing that could be checked and another that still needed an answer. Davi wrote both on the same page so that relief would not erase doubt. Their decision had moved the crisis forward, but it had also drawn the attention of Administrator Hale. By morning, the next question would reach people far beyond this room.

**繁中** 這一天沒有簡單的安慰。任知道了一件能查證的事，也留下另一件尚待回答的事。戴維把兩者寫在同一頁，免得鬆一口氣就抹掉疑問。他們的決定使危機向前，也引起黑爾管理官注意。明早下一個問題將傳到房間外更遠的地方。

**EN** Ren did not call the day a perfect victory. The machine answers understood, and no door locks, account closes, or future disappears. What endured was a decision that the people of the automated metropolis could challenge, revise, and make their own. Davi placed a red refusal key beside the public record. They left the last page open for names not yet written.

**繁中** 任不把這一天稱為完美勝利。機器回答已理解，沒有門鎖上、沒有帳戶關閉，也沒有任何未來消失。真正留下的是全自動都會人民有權質疑、修改並共同擁有的決定。戴維把一枚紅色拒絕鍵放在公共紀錄旁。他們把最後一頁留白，等待未來尚未寫上的姓名。

### 理解測驗

1. **What important problem does the opening scene reveal?**
   - A. The city cancels every investigation because nobody was harmed.
   - B. The characters learn that every record has already been destroyed.
   - C. At midnight, Ren receives a suggestion from her personal AI and simply declines it.
   **正解：C。繁中解析：** 午夜時，任收到個人 AI 的建議，平靜地按下拒絕。

2. **What changes after the new evidence appears?**
   - A. The machine answers understood, and no door locks, account closes, or future disappears.
   - B. The principal witness withdraws every statement without explanation.
   - C. The discovery guarantees that every difficulty has ended.
   **正解：A。繁中解析：** 機器回答已理解，沒有門鎖上、沒有帳戶關閉，也沒有任何未來消失。
